Targets included naval zones and major port facilities, including areas around Shahid Bahonar and Shahid Rajaee ports, as well as near an oil terminal in Bandar Abbas. A strong explosion shook [Ahvaz](599079.md) in the Zeytoun Karmandi area. An early-morning blast was reported in Songhor, a town close to the Iraqi border, while another explosion was heard near [Kazerun](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kazerun) in southern Iran. West of Tehran, multiple blasts occurred in [Malard](17437690.md) over a short time span.[^cite_note-441] In [Fardis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fardis), US–Israeli strikes targeted a helicopter manufacturing facility in the Naz industrial district. Fath Army Base in [Karaj](978698.md) was hit by strikes, as were the town's welfare offices, rehabilitation center for persons with disabilities, and residential areas. Police stations in Qom and Bandar Lengeh were also hit.[^cite_note-Human_Rights_Activists_News_Agency-2026a-442]

A video showed a heavy strike in northern Tehran on Wednesday morning.[^cite_note-443] Israel said that it targeted Iran's command centers in Tehran.[^cite_note-444] A medical center in Tehran was also hit.[^cite_note-Human_Rights_Activists_News_Agency-2026a-442] Parts of the [Iranian South Pars gas field were hit by an Israeli airstrike](82720282.md),[^cite_note-445][^cite_note-446] and the gas field's refineries in Asaluyeh were also hit.[^cite_note-Human_Rights_Activists_News_Agency-2026a-442] Israeli strikes targeted at least five Iranian naval vessels in the [Caspian Sea](19653787.md), with explosions being heard in [Bandar-e Anzali](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bandar-e_Anzali),[^cite_note-447] threatening some of the two-way weapons traffic between Russia and Iran, mainly Shahed drone equipment,[^cite_note-448] although the two countries also transport equipment through Azerbaijan.[^cite_note-449] An airstrike hit the courthouse in [Larestan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Larestan_County).[^cite_note-450] HRANA recorded 79 attacks and at least 125 casualties. At least 15 civilians were killed and 105 were injured.[^cite_note-Human_Rights_Activists_News_Agency-2026a-442]

#### Iranian strikes

Iranian missiles hit Qatar's [Ras Laffan Industrial City](24864449.md), causing fires and significant damage to the gas facility. Qatar responded by banning the Iranian embassy's military attaches from its territory.[^cite_note-451] Fragments from an Iranian missile interception damaged three private jets at [Ben Gurion Airport](113149.md).[^cite_note-452] A [missile attack](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Beit_Awwa_salon_strike) killed three Palestinians and injured 13 in [Beit Awwa](17884059.md), West Bank,[^cite_note-453] and killed a foreign worker in [Adanim](17707434.md).[^cite_note-454]

#### Other developments

The [International Maritime Organization](14986.md) (IMO) held an [extraordinary session](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Extraordinary_session) regarding [humanitarian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Humanitarian) passage for the 20,000 seafarers stranded on 3,200 vessels (both figures approximate) west of the strait.[^cite_note-455] On 18 March, thousands of civilians joined a funeral procession for Ali Larijani.[^cite_note-oj-456]

### 19 March

#### US–Israeli strikes

HRANA reported 70 attacks in 12 provinces, and at least 75 casualties including 25 civilians killed and 5 wounded. The day's strikes were the first to target the [Caspian Sea](19653787.md) region, with strikes hitting several other provinces as well, especially Iran's oil and gas facilities in Asaluyeh.[^cite_note-457] The IDF said that it destroyed an IRGC [Mil Mi-17](1493374.md) helicopter in [Sanandaj Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sanandaj_Airport).[^cite_note-458] An Israeli strike targeted [Basij](2892082.md) members. Israel said that it would continue to attack people associated with the Iranian government.[^cite_note-459] The representative of the armed forces to the [Basij](2892082.md), [Afshin Naghshbandi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Afshin_Naghshbandi?action=edit\&redlink=1), was killed in an airstrike.[^cite_note-460] An airstrike on a Basij checkpoint in [Tabriz](439791.md) killed 13 fighters and injured 18 others.[^cite_note-461]

#### Iranian strikes

A [BAZAN Group](21481272.md) refinery in Haifa was hit during a broader Iranian missile attack on the area.[^cite_note-462][^cite_note-463] Mina Al-Ahmadi refinery in Kuwait was hit by Iranian drones, causing a fire.[^cite_note-464] A US [F-35](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/F-35) was allegedly damaged by Iranian fire during a combat mission over Iran. The aircraft made an emergency landing at a US airbase in the Middle East.[^cite_note-465] IRGC published footage of the shooting.[^cite_note-466] This is considered as the first direct hit on an F-35 in history.[^cite_note-467]

#### Other developments

Netanyahu hosted a press conference asserting he was well.[^cite_note-TOI_e190-468][^cite_note-TOI_l612-469] Qatar declared the Iranian military and security [attachés](1653163.md) *[persona non grata](521241.md)*, giving them 24 hours to leave the country.[^cite_note-470]

### 20 March

#### US–Israeli strikes

HRANA recorded 640 strikes in 17 provinces, and at least 68 casualties including 4 civilians killed and 6 injured. Targets hit included Basij and army bases and garrisons in Semnan, Ahvaz, Bandar Abbas and Urmia. Residential and administrative buildings were hit in Tehran, as were the Falak-ol-Aflak Historic Site in Khorramabad, an accommodation complex in Shahmirzad and a residential building in Karaj.[^cite_note-471] IRGC spokesman [Ali Mohammad Naini](80323784.md) and his deputy were assassinated by an Israeli airstrike.[^cite_note-472][^cite_note-473] The IDF also announced that Basij intelligence chief [Ismail Ahmadi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ismail_Ahmadi?action=edit\&redlink=1) was killed in the strike that killed [Gholamreza Soleimani](61221350.md).[^cite_note-474]

#### Iranian strikes

Iran allegedly launched two ballistic missiles towards [Diego Garcia](8630.md), which houses a [US–UK military base](6004150.md), though both failed to hit the island. While one of the missile broke apart mid-flight, another was intercepted by a [SM-3](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SM-3) air defence missile launched by a US warship.[^cite_note-475][^cite_note-476] Iran later denied claims that it launched missiles towards Diego Garcia, saying that it was an "Israeli false flag" attack.[^cite_note-477]

After Iran launched nine ballistic missiles towards Jerusalem, reports indicated that at least three of the missiles were equipped with cluster munition warheads. The strikes caused damage to residential structures and resulted in minor injuries among several civilians. Debris from an intercepted missile fell within the [Old City of Jerusalem](302972.md), approximately 400 metres (1,300 ft) from the [Western Wall](75225.md) and the [Al-Aqsa Mosque](65759.md) compound on the [Temple Mount](65719.md). No injuries were reported.[^cite_note-478]

#### Other developments

Iran denied targeting Turkey and Oman, saying that they were "Israeli false flag" attacks.[^cite_note-479] [Switzerland](26748.md) announced that the country would not issue licences to export weapons by companies to the United States, citing [neutrality](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neutral_country).[^cite_note-480]

### 21 March

#### US–Israeli strikes

HRANA reported 348 attacks in 16 provinces, and at least 16 casualties including 8 civilians killed and 6 injured. The majority of the day's strikes targeted the Tehran area. Targets hit included Imam Ali Hospital in Andimeshk, a tourist complex in Ahvaz, passenger and fishing piers in Bushehr, military facilities in Tehran, Bandar Abbas, Yazd and Dezful,[^cite_note-481] and the regional headquarters of the IRGC in Isfahan.[^cite_note-482] Israeli airstrikes targeted several Tehran sites used to produce ballistic missiles.[^cite_note-483] Iran said that an airstrike hit a [uranium enrichment](37555.md) center in [Natanz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Natanz).[^cite_note-484] The IDF said that it struck an alleged nuclear weapons development and research site at Tehran's [Malek-Ashtar University of Technology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Malek-Ashtar_University_of_Technology).[^cite_note-485] A Basij site in [Najafabad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Najafabad) was "flattened" in an airstrike.[^cite_note-486]

#### Iranian strikes

Three drone strikes targeted an American diplomatic and logistics hub which houses troops in [Baghdad International Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Baghdad_International_Airport). Later, a strike in a military base in [Tuz Khurmatu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tuz_Khurmatu) killed a Hashed Al-Shaabi fighter and injured another.[^cite_note-487] Iranian missile fragments hit a kindergarten in Tel Aviv.[^cite_note-488] Later, an Iranian missile strike collapsed a building in [Dimona](9103.md), injuring 64 people, including one child critically. Iran said that it targeted [Shimon Peres Negev Nuclear Research Center](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shimon_Peres_Negev_Nuclear_Research_Center) in response to the strikes on Natanz and [Bushehr](205669.md).[^cite_note-489][^cite_note-490] A strike in [Arad](310435.md) wounded 116 people, seven seriously.[^cite_note-491]

### 22 March

#### US–Israeli strikes

HRANA recorded 206 attacks in 15 provinces with at least 4 casualties including one civilian killed and three injured. Targets hit included an airbase and naval base in Bushehr, the garrison in Garmdareh, an airbase and two power plants in Bandar Abbas, an army aviation base in Isfahan, residential buildings in Tabriz and Mohammadshahr, and the Shohada-ye Kargar Sports Complex in Masjed Soleyman.[^cite_note-492] Airstrikes took place in several other places throughout Iran, including [Ahvaz](599079.md), [Chabahar](1658958.md), [Rasht](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rasht), and [Yazd](451385.md).[^cite_note-493] A powerful early morning explosion in Tehran briefly lit up the sky in the area.[^cite_note-494] Ebrahim Mortazavi-Nasb, a [Basij](2892082.md) commander, was killed in an airstrike.[^cite_note-495] Buildings were destroyed in an airstrike in the [Alborz province](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alborz_province).[^cite_note-496]

#### Iranian and Iran-aligned strikes

Iranian missile attacks injured 15 people across central Israel.[^cite_note-497] A Hezbollah rocket strike hit a car in [Misgav Am](12960865.md), killing a man.[^cite_note-498]

### 23 March

#### US–Israeli strikes

HRANA recorded 379 attacks in 20 provinces, and at least 175 casualties including 38 civilians killed and 138 injured. A majority of the day's attacks were carried out on Tehran. Targets hit included an air force base, a gas company and residential buildings in Tehran, bases and garrisons in Garmdareh, Khorramabad and Bushehr, the Shohada-ye Pasdar School in Khorramabad and the Velayat Cultural Center in Shahr-e Rey.[^cite_note-499]

#### Iran-aligned strikes

Seven rockets were launched by a pro-Iranian Iraqi militia at a base in the [Al-Hasakah Governorate](1429110.md), which was recently vacated by American forces.[^cite_note-500]

#### Other developments

President Trump postponed US strikes against Iranian powerplants for five days amid "productive" negotiations.[^cite_note-501] Iran denied Trump's claims about productive conversations and its Foreign Ministry stated that there will be no negotiations until Iran's goals have been achieved.[^cite_note-502] Eyewitnesses in Iran described Basij patrols and security checkpoints stationed overnight throughout the country.[^cite_note-503]

### 24 March



[Mohammad Bagher Zolghadr](54308220.md) (*pictured in 2017*) was appointed secretary of the Iranian [Supreme National Security Council](6295948.md).

#### US–Israeli strikes

HRANA recorded 108 attacks in 10 provinces, and at least 61 casualties including 12 civilians killed and 38 injured. The heaviest strikes were in Khuzestan and Isfahan. Strikes hit missile sites in Tabriz and Borazjan, military industries, a gas company facility and civilian businesses in Isfahan, a gas pipeline in Khorramshahr, a sports complex in Andimeshk, and a Basij base and residential buildings in Tehran.[^cite_note-504] Airstrikes were also reported in [Shahin Shahr](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shahinshahr), [Najafabad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Najafabad), the Jowzdan industrial zone, and [Bandar Kangan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bandar_Kangan).[^cite_note-505] An overnight US strike on a base in [Al Anbar](467036.md) killed at least 15 and injured 30 members of the Iraqi [Popular Mobilization Forces](45671374.md).[^cite_note-506] The group announced the death of Saad Dawai, its operations commander in [Anbar](467036.md).[^cite_note-507] A strike also hit the home of PMF leader [Falih al-Fayyadh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Falih_Al-Fayyadh) in [Mosul](200475.md).[^cite_note-508]

#### Iranian strikes

Iranian missiles were launched at targets in [Tel Aviv](31453.md), [Eilat](66162.md), [Dimona](9103.md) and [Haifa](55606.md). Several people were injured. Kuwait reported damage to power lines due to Iranian attacks.[^cite_note-509][^cite_note-510] Iranian missiles were intercepted over [Beirut](37428.md), resulting in minor injuries.[^cite_note-511] A worker from [Morocco](19291.md) was killed and several soldiers injured during an Iranian drone attack on [Bahrain](18933277.md).[^cite_note-512]

#### Other developments

Iran appointed [Mohammad Bagher Zolghadr](54308220.md) as secretary of the [Supreme National Security Council](6295948.md) following the assassination of [Ali Larijani](531848.md).[^cite_note-513][^cite_note-514]

### 25 March

- Iranian drones set a fuel depot on Kuwait's international airport on fire, while security forces reported that another six drones were intercepted.

  [^cite_note-515]

- An Iranian missile was intercepted over [Jordan](7515964.md), with debris coming down near the capital [Amman](45007.md). The IDF reported at least one rocket intercepted.

  [^cite_note-516]

- An Iranian missile impacted on a field near Israel's largest powerplant near [Hadera](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hadera). No injuries or damages were reported.

  [^cite_note-517]

- 11 people in northwestern Iran were killed by airstrikes.

  [^cite_note-518]

- Two sites in Tehran that produced cruise missiles were destroyed by airstrikes.

  [^cite_note-519]

- Overnight strikes were reported in numerous Iranian cities, including [Chabahar](1658958.md), [Nowshahr](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nowshahr), and [Larestan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Larestan).

  [^cite_note-520]

- Israel says it destroyed a naval research center in Isfahan.

  [^cite_note-521]

- Iran claimed that it had fired [Qader cruise missiles](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Qader_\(missile\)) against [USS *Abraham Lincoln*](199106.md). The ship was not harmed.

  [^cite_note-522]

  [^cite_note-523]

- Seven [Iraqi Army](1576797.md) soldiers were killed in a U.S. airstrike near a PMF site in western Anbar, Iraq.

  [^cite_note-524]

### 26 March

#### US–Israeli strikes

- The IDF announced that Navy Commander of the IRGC, [Alireza Tangsiri](58274150.md), was killed by a strike in [Bandar Abbas](464348.md).

  [^cite_note-525]

  &#x20;This was later confirmed by the IRGC.

  [^cite_note-reuters-526]

- Iranian media reported two teenagers were killed in Shiraz during an US–Israeli attack on a residential area. Further attacks were reported from Iranian towns of Bandar Abbas, [Karaj](978698.md), [Mashhad](406512.md) and [Taybad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Taybad).

  [^cite_note-AJZ260326-527]

- The Lebanese health ministry announced that the death toll from Israeli attacks in Lebanon since the start of the invasion had risen to 1,116.

  [^cite_note-528]

#### Iranian strikes

- Iran claimed to have shot down an [F/A-18](845012.md) over [Chabahar](1658958.md). [US CENTCOM](338482.md) denied the shootdown, stating that no US aircraft had been hit by Iranian missiles.

  [^cite_note-529]

  [^cite_note-530]

- Debris from an intercepted Iranian missile killed two civilians in [Abu Dhabi](18950756.md).

  [^cite_note-AJZ260326-527]

#### Other developments

- According to the [open-source-intelligence](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Open-source-intelligence) [investigative journalism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Investigative_journalism) group [Bellingcat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bellingcat), the US apparently deployed what experts identified as BLU-91/B [antitank landmines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antitank_landmine) from a [GATOR scatterable mine system](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GATOR_scatterable_mine_system) over the village of [Kafari](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kafari?action=edit\&redlink=1), located near the southern Iranian city of [Shiraz](39622.md), early in the morning. No party in the war was known to possess the system besides the US, which had not used scatterable antitank [landmines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Landmine) in conflict since the [Gulf War](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gulf_War) in 1991 and had last used an [antipersonnel mine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antipersonnel_mine) in a single incident in 2002 during the [War in Afghanistan](19666611.md). Photographs and videos of the landmines were posted on social media by state-controlled [Islamic Republic of Iran Broadcasting](819903.md), semi-official [Tasnim News Agency](42800472.md), and Canadian independent journalist [Dimitri Lascaris](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dimitri_Lascaris), who was accompanied by representatives of the Iranian government. Tasnim News Agency reported that the landmines had caused multiple casualties and published images of them that showed the presence of aeroballistic adapters, indicating that they had been scattered by an aircraft-dropped dispenser. Lascaris said that he had seen four landmines while touring Kafari and posted footage of a bloodstain where witnesses said that a 31-year-old father had been killed by one of the landmines, which are prone to detonating when moved, after picking it up near his vehicle. The landmines may have been deployed in Kafari to prevent vehicles from accessing [Shiraz South Missile Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shiraz_South_Missile_Base?action=edit\&redlink=1), which was located about two kilometers away from some of the landmines.

  [^cite_note-TheWashingtonPost27March-531]

  [^cite_note-Bellingcat26March-532]

- Rahim Nadali, an official from the [Iranian Revolutionary Guards Corps](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iranian_Revolutionary_Guards_Corps), announced the "For Iran" initiative which allows [children as young as 12](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Child_soldiers) to be recruited into the IRGC in security-related roles.

  [^cite_note-533]

- Donald Trump claimed that the Iranian leadership was "begging" for a peace deal and declares opposing Iranian government statements as false and hinted that Iranian negotiators don't go public because they fear their own people.

  [^cite_note-534]

- Two IDF soldiers of the [Golani Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golani_Brigade) were killed in clashes with Hezbollah fighters in southern Lebanon, bringing the total of Israeli soldiers killed since the start of the invasion of Lebanon to four.

  [^cite_note-535]

  [^cite_note-536]

- Israeli prime minister Benjamin Netanyahu claimed in mid May 2026 to have met UAE leader Sheikh [Mohamed bin Zayed Al Nahyan](2121945.md) during a secret visit on 26 March in the remote desert location [Al Ain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Ain). UAE officials denied the claim.

  [^cite_note-537]

### 27 March

- IDF reported four missile salvos were fired from Iran on targets in Israel since the early morning, with most being intercepted and the rest causing no casualties.

  [^cite_note-538]

- Kuwait reported an attack by drones on [Shuwaikh Port](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shuwaikh_Port), and drones and missiles on [Mubarak Al Kabeer Port](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mubarak_Al_Kabeer_Port) causing material damage but no human casualties.

  [^cite_note-539]

  [^cite_note-540]

- UAE authorities reported six ballistic missiles and nine drones from Iran were intercepted.

  [^cite_note-541]

- Israeli strikes hit the factories of [Mobarakeh Steel Company](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mobarakeh_Steel_Company) and [Khouzestan Steel Company](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Khouzestan_Steel_Company), located respectively in [Isfahan](223149.md) and [Ahvaz](599079.md).

  [^cite_note-542]

  &#x20;Israel stated that these facilities were part of Iran's military-industrial complex and were partially controlled by the IRGC.

  [^cite_note-543]

  &#x20;Later, strikes hit a [yellowcake](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yellowcake) plant near [Yazd](451385.md) and the [IR-40](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IR-40) heavy water reactor in [Arak](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arak,_Iran).

  [^cite_note-544]

- The IDF said that it struck a major missile and [naval mine](22102.md) production site in Yazd.

  [^cite_note-545]

- An Iranian cluster bomb attack impacted six sites across central Israel, killing one person in [Ramat Gan](312761.md) and injuring three others.

  [^cite_note-546]

- An Iranian missile and drone strike on [Prince Sultan Air Base](5553272.md) damaged several US refueling aircraft and injured fifteen US soldiers, including five critically.

  [^cite_note-547]

  [^cite_note-548]

  [^cite_note-549]

  &#x20;An [E-3 Sentry](10384.md) was also damaged by the strike.

  [^cite_note-\:0-550]

  [^cite_note-551]

  [^cite_note-552]

- Iran's Ministry of Health claimed that over 230 children had been killed in US–Israeli attacks in the first month of the war.

  [^cite_note-553]

  [^cite_note-554]

### 28 March

- A [Houthi](17240332.md) ballistic missile was launched from [Yemen](350939.md) toward [Beersheba](312755.md) in Southern Israel after its military spokesman gave a speech.

  [^cite_note-555]

  [^cite_note-556]

  [^cite_note-557]

  [^cite_note-558]

  &#x20;A Houthi cruise missile targeting Israel was intercepted hours later,

  [^cite_note-559]

  &#x20;and a drone from Yemen was intercepted near [Eilat](66162.md).

  [^cite_note-560]

- Port installations in [Salalah](1518956.md) in southern Oman were hit by two drones. One worker was reported injured. Iran said that it targeted a logistics vessel belonging to US military in the vicinity of the port.

  [^cite_note-561]

  [^cite_note-562]

- Iran said that it targeted a Ukrainian anti-drone system depot in Dubai which it said was used to assist American forces.

  [^cite_note-563]

- Iranian drones hit Kuwait international airport and damaged its radar system. The fire, which had been burning in the airports fuel depot for 3 days, was finally extinguished.

  [^cite_note-564]

- The IDF said that it struck the headquarters of the [Marine Industries Organization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marine_Industries_Organization) in Tehran alongside other arms manufacturing sites and air defenses.

  [^cite_note-565]

- A drone attack targeted the home of [Kurdistan Region](3949386.md) president [Nechirvan Barzani](1110441.md) in [Duhok](566384.md).

  [^cite_note-566]

  [^cite_note-567]

- An overnight strike in Tehran damaged buildings belonging to [Iran University of Science and Technology](2073099.md). The IRGC to threatened to attack US universities in the region in response.

  [^cite_note-568]

  [^cite_note-569]

  &#x20;Later, Israeli strikes reportedly hit a water reservoir in [Haftkel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Haftkel) and killed Ali Fouladvand, the head of the [Organization of Defensive Innovation and Research](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Organization_of_Defensive_Innovation_and_Research)'s research department, in [Borujerd](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Borujerd), along with members of his family.

  [^cite_note-570]

  &#x20;Fouladvand's death was later confirmed by Iranian media.

  [^cite_note-571]

- An Iranian ballistic missile strike in [Eshtaol](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eshtaol) injured 19 people and damaged several homes.

  [^cite_note-572]

- Iranian president Masoud Pezeshkian reportedly clashed with IRGC chief-commander [Ahmad Vahidi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ahmad_Vahidi) over how the war was being conducted; he also warned that without a ceasefire, Iran's economy could collapse within three to four weeks. He criticized the IRGC's attacks on neighboring countries and called for restoration of executive powers to the civilian government.

  [^cite_note-II2803-328]

- An Iranian attack struck [Emirates Global Aluminium](12890360.md)'s Al Taweelah site in Abu Dhabi, causing damage.

  [^cite_note-ega-573]

  [^cite_note-574]

  [^cite_note-ega-573]

  [^cite_note-575]

  [^cite_note-576]

- The British navy sends the [RFA Lyme Bay](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RFA_Lyme_Bay), an amphibius ship fitted with mine-hunting drones to the Straits of Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-577]

- Iran agreed to allow 20 more Pakistani-flagged ships to transit Strait of Hormuz; Islamabad confirmed it.

  [^cite_note-578]

- An IDF soldier of the [Paratroopers Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/35th_Paratroopers_Brigade_\(Israel\)) was killed in a Hezbollah rocket attack in southern Lebanon, increasing the number of IDF soldiers killed since the start of the invasion to 5.

  [^cite_note-579]

### 29 March

- Overnight drone attacks in [Erbil](24534207.md), Iraq, targeted the home of [Kurdistan Democratic Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kurdistan_Democratic_Party) leader [Masoud Barzani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Masoud_Barzani), Iranian opposition headquarters, and a US base near [Erbil International Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Erbil_International_Airport).

  [^cite_note-580]

- Explosions were reported in many places in Iran including Abad, Andisheh, District 15, Evin, Masoudieh, Nobonyad, Qaleh Hasan Khan, and Saadat.

  [^cite_note-581]

- Israel said it struck mobile command centers and places used to produce weapons in Tehran.

  [^cite_note-582]

  [^cite_note-583]

- An Iranian missile struck an [ADAMA](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adama_Agricultural_Solutions) chemical facility in [Ne'ot Hovav](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ne'ot_Hovav), injuring one person.

  [^cite_note-584]

  [^cite_note-585]

  [^cite_note-586]

- Power was cut in parts of Tehran and [Alborz province](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alborz_province), including [Karaj](978698.md), after electrical infrastructure was damaged by shrapnel.

- Late on 29 March Iranian drones hit a military installation in Kuwait injuring 10 soldiers.

  [^cite_note-AZ300326-589]
