  [^cite_note-607]

  [^cite_note-608]

- The IDF said that it killed the commander of the [Quds Force](4028355.md) Lebanon Corps' engineering branch in an airstrike in [Mahallat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mahallat), adding that he led the construction of dozens of underground structures for Hezbollah and the [Assad regime](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assad_regime).

  [^cite_note-609]

- Iran confirmed the death of brigadier general [Jamshid Eshaghi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jamshid_Eshaghi), who led the [Iranian General Staff](50548383.md)'s budgeting and financial affairs and was killed alongside members of his family.

  [^cite_note-610]

## April

### 1 April

- A fuel storage at Kuwait International Airport was hit by Iranian drones, causing a major fire.

  [^cite_note-611]

- Israel reported four missile barrages fired from Iran, with impacts of cluster munition reported from Bnei Brak and Tel Aviv, injuring 14 people.

  [^cite_note-TOI010426-612]

- IDF reported a missile launched by Houthi militias was intercepted.

  [^cite_note-TOI010426-612]

- Several waves of drones targeted a factory which produced motor oils in [Erbil](24534207.md) Iraq, causing a major fire.

  [^cite_note-613]

- A US strike in Tehran reportedly damaged the [former US embassy](24966434.md), which was converted into a museum following its takeover during the [Iranian Revolution](347268.md).

  [^cite_note-614]

  &#x20;The [St. Nicholas Orthodox Church](27326879.md) was also damaged.

  [^cite_note-615]

- A claim by Donald Trump that the Iranian president asked for a [ceasefire](592591.md) was rejected by Iran as "false and baseless".

  [^cite_note-616]

- Two US [MQ-9 Reaper](4769988.md) drones were shot down by Iran near [Isfahan](223149.md), bringing the total number of MQ-9s lost during the war to 16.

  [^cite_note-617]

  [^cite_note-618]

- Iranian outlet Nournews reported that former foreign minister [Kamal Kharazi](186801.md) was seriously injured and his wife was killed in an airstrike which hit his home.

  [^cite_note-619]

- Mohammad Ali Fathalizadeh, commander of the IRGC's [Fatehin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islamic_Revolutionary_Guard_Corps_Ground_Forces#Divisions_and_Brigades) unit, was killed by an airstrike.

  [^cite_note-620]

- US president Donald Trump delivered [a speech](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File\:President%20Trump%20Delivers%20an%20Address%20to%20the%20Nation,%20Apr.%201,%202026.webm) stating the war's objectives were "nearing completion," projecting it could last a few more weeks, and threatening to bomb Iran "back to the [stone age](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stone_Age)" if it did not agree to reopen the Strait of Hormuz and ease disruptions to global energy markets.

  [^cite_note-621]

  [^cite_note-622]

&#x20; \


### 2 April

- The IDF said that it struck an [IRGC Ground Forces](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IRGC_Ground_Forces) base and mobile command centers in Tehran.

  [^cite_note-623]

  &#x20;Two US strikes on the [Karaj B1 bridge](82859106.md) between Tehran and [Karaj](978698.md), which was described as the highest bridge in the Middle East, reportedly killed eight people and caused it to collapse.

  [^cite_note-624]

- Iraq began exporting crude by tanker trucks through Syria to Mediterranean ports such as [Baniyas](30871895.md).

  [^cite_note-625]

- Buildings of the [Pasteur Institute of Iran](41907959.md) were destroyed by an air attack.

  [^cite_note-626]

- A fuel tank near [Mashhad International Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mashhad_International_Airport) was struck by a projectile, sparking a fire.

  [^cite_note-627]

- An Iranian strike targeted an [Amazon](90451.md) [cloud computing](19541494.md) facility in Bahrain, causing a fire.

  [^cite_note-628]

  &#x20;Iran also said it attacked US fighter jets at Jordan's [Al Azraq Air Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Azraq_Air_Base) with drones.

  [^cite_note-629]

  &#x20;The IRGC later said that it targeted an [Oracle](22591.md) data center in Dubai, although the city denied this.

  [^cite_note-630]

  [^cite_note-631]

- The IDF said that it killed the commander of Iran's ballistic missiles unit in [Kermanshah](1489244.md) and three other battalion commanders.

  [^cite_note-632]

- An Iranian ballistic missile strike damaged an Israeli drone factory in [Petah Tikva](202411.md).

  [^cite_note-633]

  [^cite_note-634]

  [^cite_note-635]

### 3 April



A US-crewed [McDonnell Douglas F-15E Strike Eagle](495724.md) (*example pictured*) was shot down over Iran.

- Iranian drones hit the [Mina Al-Ahmadi refinery](2922077.md) in Kuwait and set it on fire in multiple locations.

  [^cite_note-636]

- Explosions were reported in many places across Iran, including Alborz, Bushehr, Fars, Isfahan, Khuzestan, and Tehran.

  [^cite_note-637]

- Kuwait authorities reported Iranian drones damaged a desalination plant.

  [^cite_note-AZ030426-638]

- Abu Dhabi media office reported debris from an interception caused a fire in the [Habshan](8495134.md) gas facility.

  [^cite_note-AZ030426-638]

  &#x20;An Egyptian citizen was killed and four other foreign nationals were injured by interception debris.

  [^cite_note-639]

- [Iranian forces shot down](82877513.md) a US Air Force [F-15E Strike Eagle](495724.md) fighter jet over the country, prompting a search and rescue mission by US helicopters.

  [^cite_note-640]

  &#x20;Both of the two crew members were rescued by US forces.

  [^cite_note-641]

  &#x20;Two US [MC-130 Hercules](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lockheed_MC-130), an A-10 aircraft and 4 helicopters were destroyed in the operation.

  [^cite_note-642]

- A US [A-10 Warthog](12502446.md) crashed into the [Persian Gulf](24761.md), near the [Strait of Hormuz](29005.md). The pilot was rescued.

  [^cite_note-643]

- Iranian cluster munitions caused extensive damage in [Kiryat Ata](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kiryat_Ata) and injured one person.

  [^cite_note-ytn030426-644]

- Israel claims to have killed Iranian missile commander Makram Azimi in [Kermanshah province](448078.md).

  [^cite_note-645]

- The Israeli government extended operational reserve duty for current and future reservists to approximately nine weeks.

  [^cite_note-ytn030426-644]

- [Emirates Global Aluminium](12890360.md) reported that an Iranian attack on its plant in Abu Dhabi caused significant damage and may take up to a year to restore its full production.

  [^cite_note-646]

- Iranian media reported that Iran executed two men linked to [Mojahedin-e-Khalq](285632.md) for planning to carry out attacks across the country.

  [^cite_note-647]

- Israel says it killed Mohammadreza Ashrafi, senior IRGC official responsible for the oil headquarters.

  [^cite_note-648]

### 4 April

- Iranian missile attacks on Israel with cluster munitions damaging buildings and roads were reported from Bnei Brak, [Ramat Gan](312761.md), Tel Aviv and [Petah Tikva](202411.md).

  [^cite_note-649]

  &#x20;One of those cluster bombs hit in the vicinity of [the IDF's Kirya headquarters in Tel Aviv](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HaKirya).

  [^cite_note-650]

  &#x20;The home of [United Torah Judaism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Torah_Judaism) leader [Moshe Gafni](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moshe_Gafni) in [Bnei Brak](314671.md) was also hit by a cluster munition.

  [^cite_note-651]

- Iranian media reported an air attack on a cement factory in [Bandar Khamir](24750251.md).

  [^cite_note-652]

- A strike hit a building near the [Bushehr Nuclear Power Plant](18522794.md) in Iran, killing one of its security guards.

  [^cite_note-653]

  &#x20;Israeli strikes also hit [petrochemical](67728.md) sites in Khuzestan province, injuring five people.

  [^cite_note-654]

  &#x20;Iranian press reported US–Israeli strikes did hit the [Bandar-e Mahshahr](1851356.md) and Bandar Imam petrochemical zones as well as petrochemical industries in Fajr, Rejal, and Amir-Kabir.

  [^cite_note-655]

  &#x20;Five people were killed and 170 others were injured in the strikes in Mahshahr, according to Iranian media.

  [^cite_note-656]

- Iran claimed that it struck the container ship *MSC Ishyka* with a drone in the Strait of Hormuz, alleging that it was linked to Israel. The attack was not independently verified.

  [^cite_note-657]

- An airstrike hit the Iranian side of the [Shalamcheh](8247289.md) border crossing, killing an Iraqi citizen and seriously injuring five others. Iraq closed the crossing in response.

  [^cite_note-658]

### 5 April

- After heavy fighting, the missing [weapon systems officer](4072511.md) (WSO) from the downed F-15E was [successfully recovered](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_U.S._pilot_rescue_operation_in_Iran) by US commandos.

  [^cite_note-wso-recovered-659]

  [^cite_note-660]

  [^cite_note-661]

  &#x20;The temporary US [forward operating base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Forward_operating_base) in Iran, established to rescue the WSO, was evacuated and two [C-130s](7697.md) and at least one [MH-6 Little Bird](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MH-6_Little_Bird) were left behind and destroyed by the retreating US forces.

  [^cite_note-662]

- Explosions and overhead flights were reported overnight in many provinces, including [Alborz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alborz_province), [Boyer-Ahmad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boyer-Ahmad_County), [Bushehr](205669.md), [Fars](448049.md), [Gilan](210689.md), [Hormozgan](448064.md), [Isfahan](448061.md), [Khuzestan](448079.md), [Kohgiluyeh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kohgiluyeh_County), and [Mazandaran](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mazandaran_province).

  [^cite_note-663]

- Iranian drones hit a [BAPCO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bahrain_Petroleum_Company) oil storage facility in [Bahrain](18933277.md) and set it on fire. Further drone attacks hit the Shuwaikh Oil Sector Complex and a desalination plant in Kuwait.

  [^cite_note-664]

- The IDF said that it killed Mohammad Reza Ashrafi Kahi, who was the Head of Commerce at the IRGC Oil Headquarters.

  [^cite_note-665]

- An Iranian missile strike hit a residential building in [Haifa](55606.md), killing four people. Nine people were injured in the attack, one seriously.

  [^cite_note-haifamisl4-666]

  [^cite_note-667]

- Iran announced that the number of attacks on civilian centers in Israeli and American attacks (on Iran) was: 83,351 residential units.

  [^cite_note-yjc-9072529-668]

  &#x20;Also, 322 medical centers and 763 schools (in Iran) have been attacked so far.

  [^cite_note-yjc-9072529-668]

### 6 April

US president Donald Trump on 6 April 2026&#x20;

- An Iranian missile barrage early in the morning impacted 15 areas, seriously injuring a woman in [Petah Tikva](202411.md) and injuring two others in Tel Aviv.

  [^cite_note-669]

  &#x20;Four people were lightly wounded by a cluster bomb in [Haifa](55606.md).

  [^cite_note-670]

- An airstrike hit two homes in [Baharestan County](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Baharestan_County), [Tehran province](202698.md), killing 23 people.

  [^cite_note-671]

  &#x20;Overnight strikes in [Bandar Lengeh](1337157.md) and [Kong](32900800.md) killed six people and injured 17 others.

  [^cite_note-672]

  &#x20;Five others were killed in [Qom](638742.md). Tehran's [Sharif University of Technology](453155.md) was also struck.

  [^cite_note-673]

- Debris from a missile interception fell on a Raneen Systems building in [Mussafah](39657415.md), UAE, injuring a Ghanaian.

  [^cite_note-674]

- Explosions and attacks in the early morning were reported at a military site in northern Iran and in [Khorramshahr](464970.md).

  [^cite_note-675]

- IRGC intelligence chief [Majid Khademi](80084010.md) was killed in an American-Israeli airstrike.

  [^cite_note-676]

  &#x20;Israel also said that it killed [Yazdan Mir](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yazdan_Mir?action=edit\&redlink=1), the commander of the Quds Force's Unit 840.

  [^cite_note-677]

- Iran said that it targeted US satellite equipment and munitions on [Bubiyan Island](1550688.md), Kuwait using drones.

  [^cite_note-678]

- Four bodies were found during a search and rescue operation on a destroyed house hit by an Iranian missile in Haifa the day before.

  [^cite_note-haifamisl4-666]

- Israeli Minister of Defense [Israel Katz](19041378.md) said that the IDF struck Iran's most prominent petrochemical plant in [Asaluyeh](3393482.md), causing major damage.

  [^cite_note-679]

- The IDF said that it struck [Mehrabad International Airport](2025041.md) and two smaller airfields, destroying dozens of Iranian airplanes and helicopters.

  [^cite_note-680]

- Iraqi security sources said that two people were killed after an Iranian drone strike on the village of [Zarka Zoy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zarka_Zoy?action=edit\&redlink=1), in the [Darashakran district](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Darashakran_district?action=edit\&redlink=1) of [Erbil Governorate](463860.md).

  [^cite_note-681]

- 15 Americans were wounded in an overnight Iranian drone strike on [Ali Al Salem Air Base](1469367.md).

  [^cite_note-682]

- Trump threatened to send Iran 'back to the Stone Ages' if it does not reach a deal by the ultimatum he set.

  [^cite_note-683]

- Up to 6 April, as a result of the US and Israeli attacks, damage(s) has also been done to sports federations and sports infrastructure in Iran. Among the damages are the following: the complete destruction of the 12,000-seat [Azadi Hall](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Azadi_Stadium), the camp of the [national wrestling teams of the Iran](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wrestling_in_Iran), various parts of the Shooting Federation, parts of the Tennis Federation, the Hockey Federation building located in the Tehran Revolution Sports Complex, and the cycling and handball federations in Iran.

  [^cite_note-sports-336]

### 7 April

| Avatar of Donald J. TrumpAvatar of Donald J. Trump | **Donald J. Trump** (@[realDonaldTrump](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RealDonaldTrump)) tweeted: |
| --- | --- |

[English](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/English_language):&#x20;

> A whole civilization will die tonight, never to be brought back again. I don’t want that to happen, but it probably will. However, now that we have Complete and Total Regime Change, where different, smarter, and less radicalized minds prevail, maybe something revolutionarily wonderful can happen, WHO KNOWS? We will find out tonight, one of the most important moments in the long and complex history of the World. 47 years of extortion, corruption, and death, will finally end. God Bless the Great People of Iran!

April 7, 2026[^cite_note-684]

- Iranian drones hit a [SABIC](1929210.md) petrochemical complex in [Jubail](632008.md) in Saudi Arabia, causing a fire.

  [^cite_note-685]

- Overnight strikes were reported in the greater Tehran area, in [Ahvaz](599079.md), [Chabahar](1658958.md), [Jask](19973566.md), [Shiraz](39622.md), and [Yazd](451385.md), and near the Shahid Kalantari port, Tis port, and the Imam Ali base.

  [^cite_note-686]

- The [Rafi'-Nia synagogue](82896351.md) in central Tehran was destroyed in an airstrike.

  [^cite_note-687]

  &#x20;The IDF admitted to conducting the strike, stating that it targeted a commander in the [Khatam al-Anbiya Central Headquarters](51128846.md) and regretted damage to the synagogue.

  [^cite_note-688]

- The US said that it conducted strikes against military sites on [Kharg Island](2210066.md), with Iranian media reporting several large explosions.

  [^cite_note-689]

  [^cite_note-690]

- A highway bridge connecting [Hashtrud](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hashtrud) and [Tabriz](439791.md) and a rail bridge in [Kashan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kashan) was hit in an airstrike, killing two people.

  [^cite_note-691]

  &#x20;The IDF later said that it bombed eight bridges across Iran used to transport military equipment.

  [^cite_note-692]

- At least five people were killed after a rocket fired from Kuwait landed in Basra, southern Iraq.

  [^cite_note-693]

- Iran distributes 180,000 [iodine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iodine) tablets to [Bushehr](205669.md) residents as part of a crisis preparedness plan.

  [^cite_note-694]

- Trump said he agreed to a [two-week ceasefire](82888027.md) with Iran in exchange for the immediate reopening of the [Strait of Hormuz](29005.md).

  [^cite_note-695]

- Pakistani prime minister [Shehbaz Sharif](6502561.md) announced an immediate ceasefire "everywhere, including Lebanon and elsewhere".

  [^cite_note-AZ0804-696]

### 8 April

- Israeli prime minister [Benjamin Netanyahu](56472.md) announced on social media that the ceasefire does not include Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-AZ0804-696]

- Early morning, Iranian agencies reported explosions from [Sirri Island](9169338.md) and [Lavan Island](1954991.md) of unknown origin.

  [^cite_note-697]

  &#x20;The strike on Lavan island was later attributed to the UAE by press research from [The Wall Street Journal](173070.md) in mid May 2026.

  [^cite_note-698]

- Iranian missiles and drones were intercepted over the UAE, and Iranian drones also targeted energy infrastructure and desalination plants in Kuwait, causing serious damage. Two people were injured by shrapnel in [Sitra](4006219.md), Bahrain.

  [^cite_note-699]

- The Israeli air force launched the biggest wave of attacks against targets in Lebanon since the beginning of the conflict, hitting more than 100 sites.

  [^cite_note-700]

  &#x20;At least 254 people were killed and 837 injured.

  [^cite_note-701]

  &#x20;Hezbollah claimed responsibility for launching rockets towards north Israel as a response to "cease-fire violations".

  [^cite_note-702]

- Iranian media said that Iran paused Hormuz traffic over Israeli attacks in Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-703]

- Masoud Pezeshkian said the ceasefire with the United States was broken as [Lavan](1954991.md) and [Siri](9169338.md) islands were bombed.

  [^cite_note-704]

- Donald Trump claimed after the Israeli air assault on Lebanon, that the Pakistani Prime Minister's announcement of the peace deal was wrong and Israeli interpretation of the deal was correct and Lebanon was excluded. Iranian news agency Tasnim on the other hand reported that Iran will withdraw from the agreement if Israel attacks on Lebanon continue.

  [^cite_note-705]

  &#x20;US vice president [JD Vance](53396477.md) stated that there had been a "legitimate misunderstanding" about the inclusion of Lebanon, with Israel having promised to hold back a bit in Lebanon to help with the negotiations, but it was not part of the ceasefire.

  [^cite_note-706]

### 9 April

- Kuwait announced that it was intercepting a drone attack on its territory that was targeting vital institutions.

  [^cite_note-707]

  &#x20;The [Kuwait National Guard](45563700.md) later announced that one of its sites were attacked, causing damage.

  [^cite_note-708]

  &#x20;Iran denied responsibility.

  [^cite_note-709]

- The US said that an Iran-backed militia conducted drone attacks near the [Baghdad Diplomatic Support Center](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Baghdad_Diplomatic_Support_Center) and [Baghdad International Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Baghdad_International_Airport).

  [^cite_note-710]

- Former Iranian foreign minister [Kamal Kharazi](186801.md) succumbed to wounds sustained by an airstrike on his home nine days prior.

  [^cite_note-711]

- The 40th-day commemoration of the passing of the former leader of the Islamic Republic of Iran, Ali Khamenei, was held with the participation of people and groups of mourners from inside and outside the country.

  [^cite_note-712]

  [^cite_note-713]

### 10 April

- According to Iranian media [Mehr News Agency](2707394.md), till 10 April 2026, 203 Iranian sports venues, including indoor arenas, swimming pools, artificial turf fields, and office buildings, have been damaged in US–Israeli attacks, with damage levels estimated to range from 2 to 100 percent.

  [^cite_note-714]

### 11 April

- Trump said that American forces started "[clearing](82741240.md)" the [Strait of Hormuz](29005.md).

  [^cite_note-715]

  &#x20;Iran claimed that an American vessel on way to the strait turned back after warning.

  [^cite_note-716]

  &#x20;*[The Wall Street Journal](173070.md)* reported that several US Navy [destroyers](8779.md) entered the Strait of Hormuz for the first time since the war began. A US official described the event as an "operation that focused on freedom of navigation through International waters". Iranian government reportedly threatened to attack the ships, accusing US of ceasefire violation.

  [^cite_note-717]

  [^cite_note-718]

  &#x20;US [Central Command](338482.md) said the ships are taking part in [mine clearance](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mine_clearance) operations.

  [^cite_note-719]

### 12 April

- Early morning, local time in [Islamabad](51153.md), after some 20 hours of negotiations, US vice president [JD Vance](53396477.md) stated that Iranian negotiators did not accept the terms the US representatives JD Vance, [Jared Kushner](6191053.md) and [Steve Witkoff](40901053.md) had laid out and that the talks have failed.

  [^cite_note-720]

- On his social media platform, Trump declared [a naval blockade](82937256.md) on the Strait of Hormuz, announcing that the US Navy will prevent ships from entering or exiting the Strait and intercept vessels that have paid tolls to Iran.

  [^cite_note-721]

  &#x20;[United States Central Command](338482.md) announced that the blockade will be enforced on vessels of all nations entering or departing Iranian ports and coastal areas but "will not impede freedom of navigation for vessels transiting the Strait of Hormuz to and from non-Iranian ports."

  [^cite_note-722]

### 13 April

- According to Donald Trump, the US [Naval blockade of Iran](82937256.md) was due to start at 13 April 10\:00 a.m. [ET](496028.md) (2\:00 p.m. UTC).

  [^cite_note-723]

### 14 April

- The director of the [Hind Rajab Foundation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hind_Rajab_Foundation), reported that [Daniel Ella](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daniel_Ella?action=edit\&redlink=1), commander of the 52nd Battalion of the [401st Brigade](2072251.md) which was involved in the [killing of Hind Rajab](76056228.md), was seriously injured in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-724]

- Iran was reported to have been digging underground to retrieve missile launchers.

  [^cite_note-725]

  [^cite_note-726]

### 15 April

- Donald Trump declared he is permanently opening the Strait of Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-727]

### 16 April

- China's foreign minister [Wang Yi](18211536.md) told his Iranian counterpart, that opening the Strait of Hormuz was of "unanimous interest".

  [^cite_note-728]

### 17 April

- Iranian foreign minister announced that passage of commercial vessels through the Strait of Hormuz would be permitted during the [truce](82970933.md) in Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-729]

### 18 April

- Iran said that it reasserted control of Hormuz as a result of the US refusing to lift [its naval blockade](82937256.md).

  [^cite_note-730]

- [UKMTO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UKMTO) reported two Iranian gunboats opened fire on a tanker that tried to pass through the Strait of Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-731]

### 19 April

- An explosive device was detonated in southern Lebanon, killing an Israeli soldier and injuring nine others.

  [^cite_note-732]

- An announcement by Donald Trump of a new round of talks in Islamabad is dismissed as false by Iran's [Tasnim News Agency](42800472.md) based on informed sources.

  [^cite_note-733]

- The US intercepted [an Iranian cargo ship](82992016.md) in the Strait of Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-734]

  &#x20;Iran claimed this was a 'ceasefire violation' and promised a response.

  [^cite_note-735]

  &#x20;Iran retaliated against the seizure by firing drones at US ships, although no damage was reported.

  [^cite_note-736]

### 21 April

- Donald Trump extended the Iran truce to allow time for an Iranian proposal to be submitted at Pakistan's request.

  [^cite_note-737]

### 22 April

- Iranian Revolutionary Guards gunboats engaged three container vessels, the *Epaminodes* (IMO 9153862), *Euphoria* (IMO 9235828) and *MSC-Francesca* (IMO 9401116) in the Strait of Hormuz. *Epaminodes* and *MSC-Francesca* were seized and escorted to the coast of Iran, claiming safety violations.

  [^cite_note-738]

  &#x20;*Euphoria* reported heavy damage to the bridge by gun and rocketfire from Iranian gunboats.

  [^cite_note-739]

### 23 April

- Trump ordered the US Navy to destroy any Iranian boats laying mines in the Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-740]

- President Trump announced that Israel and Lebanon agreed to a three-week extension of the [ceasefire](82970933.md).

  [^cite_note-741]

- Tasnim reported that Iranian security forces destroyed a [People's Fighters Front](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/People's_Fighters_Front) (PFF) operational cell, with several militants there that previously infiltrated the Pakistani border to [Rask](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rask,_Iran) said to be killed by the Iranian news agency.

  [^cite_note-742]

### 24 April

- Iran said that it confiscated a vessel suspected of collaborating with American forces after the vessel committed violations and ignored warnings.

  [^cite_note-743]

- Kuwait's defense ministry reported that two northern Kuwaiti border outposts were struck by [fiber optic drones](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fiber_optic_drone) from Iraq, causing material damage but no casualties.

  [^cite_note-744]

## May

### 3 May

- Trump announced that [the US will help free up ships in the Strait of Hormuz](83096724.md) beginning the next morning.

  [^cite_note-745]

### 4 May

- US Central Command announced two US-flagged ships, coming from the Gulf, passed the Strait of Hormuz unchallenged, while two US warships passed from the east.

  [^cite_note-marex040526-746]

- Two civilian ship reported attacks near the Strait of Hormuz, South Korean container vessel *HMM Namu* was damaged near [Umm Al Quwain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Umm_Al_Quwain), while a tanker reported an incident near Dubai.

  [^cite_note-marex040526-746]

- Iranian state media claimed that the Iran's navy launched two missiles and hit an US naval frigate in the Strait of Hormuz. US Central Command denied that the projectiles struck the vessel.

  [^cite_note-747]

  [^cite_note-748]

- American forces reportedly destroyed six or seven small Iranian boats in the Strait of Hormuz during a clash. The boats were destroyed after they allegedly tried to interfere with the transit of two US flagged commercial vessels and two Navy destroyer vessels ([USS *Truxtun*](765604.md) and [USS *Mason*](765529.md)).

  [^cite_note-749]

- UAE officials state that Iran attacked the oil Industry Zone in the port of [Fujairah](58033142.md) with drones, setting several storage tanks on fire and injuring several workers. Four missiles were also launched by Iran according to the UAE, but those were destroyed. The Fujairah port is used to export oil without having to use the Strait of Hormuz. Omani officials also reported Iranian strikes in their territory.

  [^cite_note-750]

  [^cite_note-751]

### 5 May

- UAE's ministry of defence stated the country was again defending against a barrage of Iranian missiles and drones.

  [^cite_note-752]

- The [CMA CGM](1903569.md) line stated that one of their freighters, *CMA GGM San Antonio*, was attacked in the Strait of Hormuz late on 5 May. Several crewmembers were injured and were evacuated for treatment.

  [^cite_note-753]

### 6 May

- President Trump temporary stopped the US military's "Project Freedom" operation to reopen the Strait of Hormuz, citing "great progress" at a potential deal with Iran. Trump added that the operation will be halted for "a short period" to determine whether a "complete and final agreement" with Iran could be achieved.

  [^cite_note-754]

- US Central Command stated that US aircraft used cannon fire to disable the rudder on the Iranian tanker MT *Hasna* because it had tried to break the blockade and reach Iran.

  [^cite_note-755]

### 7 May

- The US said that it [carried out strikes on Iranian military sites](83096724.md) in [Bandar Abbas](464348.md) and [Qeshm Island](2252168.md) in response to an earlier combined missile, drone, and small boat attack on American destroyers crossing the Strait of Hormuz. Iran accused the US of violating the truce by targeting an Iranian oil tanker and another vessel. It also claimed that the US air strikes hit civilian areas in Qeshm Island, and coastal areas in its vicinity in [Bandar Khamir](24750251.md) and [Sirik](25145628.md). The military said it targeted American military vessels east of the Strait of Hormuz and south of the [Chabahar Port](15313649.md) in response to these events.

  [^cite_note-756]

  [^cite_note-757]

- The UAE said that its air defense systems intercepted two Iranian ballistic missiles and three drones, moderately wounding three people.

  [^cite_note-758]

- The US said that it conducted strikes on two empty oil tankers allegedly trying to reach Iran.

  [^cite_note-759]

- Iranian officials stated that six individuals went missing and several others were wounded in US attacks which hit "Iranian fishing and cargo vessels" in the vicinity of [Khasab](1658322.md).

  [^cite_note-760]

### 8 May

- Iranian state TV reported that the tanker *Ocean Koi* (IMO 9255933) was seized by Iranian forces.

  [^cite_note-761]

- US Central Command reported it fired on and disabled Iranian tankers MT *Sea Star III* and MT *Sevda* running the blockade.

  [^cite_note-762]

### 9 May

- Security forces in Bahrain arrested 41 people, accused of conducting espionage on behalf of the Iranian IRGC.

  [^cite_note-763]

### 10 May

- UAE and Kuwait reported drone incursions which were neutralized, and a fire allegedly broke out on a ship off Qatar after a drone strike.

  [^cite_note-764]

### 13 May

- The Indian-flagged small freighter *Haji Ali* reported to have been stricken by a drone or missile off the coast of Oman and sank. All 14 crewmembers were rescued by Omani authorities.

  [^cite_note-765]

### 14 May

- UKMTO reported unauthorized personnel seized an unidentified ship 38 nautical miles (70 km; 44 mi) off [Fujairah](58033142.md) and moved it towards Iran. The ship was later identified as the Honduras-flagged *Hui Chuan*, which apparently functioned as a floating armory, storing weapons for [PMC's](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Private_military_company).

  [^cite_note-766]

### 17 May

- A drone strike caused a fire at the edge of the UAE's [Barakah nuclear power plant](39806131.md). No injuries or radiation leaks were reported.

  [^cite_note-767]

- The Iranian police chief, [Ahmad-Reza Radan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ahmad-Reza_Radan), has said that more than 6,500 "traitors and spies" have been arrested since the start of the war.

  [^cite_note-768]

### 18 May

- The *New York Times* reported that the IDF operated two "covert" outposts in Iraq's western desert and killed a shepherd and a soldier in a bid to hide one of the sites near the town of [al-Nukhaib](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Nukhaib).

  [^cite_note-769]

### 22 May

- [Qatar assumed a mediator role in the war](76142543.md), despite suffering [Iranian attacks](82557695.md). However, Pakistan remained the primary mediator.

  [^cite_note-reuters22may-770]

### 24 May

- Iranian media reported that the Iranian military intercepted an Israeli [Orbiter](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aeronautics_Defense_Orbiter) surveillance drone in its [Hormozgan province](448064.md). Israel said that it was unaware of any such incident.

  [^cite_note-771]

### 25 May

- Iranian president [Masoud Pezeshkian](2650383.md) ordered to end the internet blackout.

  [^cite_note-772]

- The [US Central Command](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/US_Central_Command) reported "self-defense" strikes on Iran, saying that [the ceasefire](82888027.md) is still in place, with several Iranian soldiers reportedly killed. Qatari news broadcaster *[Al Jazeera](30204052.md)* reported that unnamed Iranian sources informed them, that the IRGC targeted a ship at sea before the US attacks.

  [^cite_note-773]

  [^cite_note-774]

### 26 May

- Israeli troops move north of the Litani river in Lebanon and fights are reported in [Zawtar al-Sharqiyah](34789989.md). IDF reported 100 strikes in the [Beqaa Valley](2162385.md).

  [^cite_note-775]

### 27 May

- US forces conducted another attack at a base on Iran's southern coastline. Iranian forces launched several drones, four of which were allegedly intercepted.

  [^cite_note-776]

### 28 May

- Kuwait downed a ballistic missile launched at its territory. Iran said that it targeted the American base in Kuwait responsible for the attack on the previous day.

  [^cite_note-777]

  &#x20;*[Bloomberg News](3057255.md)* reported, citing a person familiar with the incident that debris from the downed [Fateh-110](4603470.md) missile fell on [Ali Al Salem Air Base](1469367.md), slightly injuring roughly five American soldiers and contractors and destroying one [US MQ-9 Reaper drone](4769988.md) and causing serious damage to at least one more.

  [^cite_note-778]

## June

### 1 June

- After increased US strikes on Iran and Israeli attacks in Lebanon, Iran announced to end all peace talks with the US.

  [^cite_note-779]

- Israel promised not to hit Beirut's southern suburbs, and Hezbollah pledged not to attack Israel as part of a US proposal, with the cease-fire framework to be expanded to include the entirety of Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-780]

### 2 June

- US forces attacked the tanker M/T *Lexie* while it was en route to Kharg Island, after it ignored orders to stay away from Iran. The ship was hit by a missile, intended to disable it.

  [^cite_note-Guardian040626-781]

### 3 June

- Kuwait International Airport was hit by an Iranian drone, killing one and injuring 63.

  [^cite_note-Guardian040626-781]

### 7 June

- Israeli forces did strike the southern suburbs of Lebanon's capital Beirut.

  [^cite_note-782]

  &#x20;Lebanese ⁠state media said that an Israeli strike in the southern suburbs of Beirut killed two people and wounded 11 others. The IDF said that it targeted Hezbollah infrastructure after the group fired towards northern Israel.

  [^cite_note-783]

  &#x20;The same day, Iran said that American bases and Israeli assets in Middle East are legitimate targets due to the American naval blockade against the country and Israeli strikes on [Beirut](37428.md)'s southern suburbs, where neighborhoods that Hezbollah members live in are located.

  [^cite_note-784]

  &#x20;Iran later launched ballistic missiles towards Israel.

  [^cite_note-785]

  &#x20;Israel said that it downed all the missiles.

  [^cite_note-786]

  &#x20;Iran said that it targeted [Ramat David Airbase](5340731.md).

  [^cite_note-787]

  &#x20;Israel said that it will retaliate "forcefully" to the alleged Iranian violation of the truce.

  [^cite_note-788]

  &#x20;The next day, the IDF announced that it struck military sites in central and western Iran, with explosions being reported in Tehran, Isfahan, and Tabriz.

  [^cite_note-789]

  &#x20;This triggered an exchange of strikes between the two countries. The Houthis also joined the strikes.

  [^cite_note-790]

  &#x20;Two Iranian soldiers were killed in an Israeli attack.

  [^cite_note-791]

  &#x20;Iran later says that it had ceased military actions against Israel.

  [^cite_note-792]

  &#x20;Israel later announces that it stopped Iran strikes at President Trump's request.

  [^cite_note-793]

- Iranian military forces attack People's Fighters Front militants in [Saravan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Saravan,_Iran), resulting in the deaths of four PFF fighters and one IRGC soldier.

  [^cite_note-794]

### 9 June

- A US Army [AH-64 Apache](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AH-64_Apache) attack helicopter crashed off the coast of Oman after a collision with an Iranian [Shahed drone](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shahed_drone). Trump blamed Iran and pledged retaliation;

  [^cite_note-CBS-9-June-795]

  &#x20;Iran denied the collision was intentional.

  [^cite_note-796]

  &#x20;The crew was rescued by a [Saronic Technologies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Saronic_Technologies?action=edit\&redlink=1) [sea drone](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sea_drone), reportedly the first US Navy search-and-rescue operation with a sea drone.

  [^cite_note-797]

  &#x20;CENTCOM said the incident was under investigation.

  [^cite_note-CBS-9-June-795]

- CENTCOM subsequently said that it launched retaliatory strikes against Iran.

  [^cite_note-798]

### 10 June

- Iran said that it launched strikes aimed at [US Fifth Fleet](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/US_Fifth_Fleet), [Ali Al Salem airbase](1469367.md) and launched missiles towards [American base in Jordan](32592197.md) housing [F-35s](11812.md) in retaliation to the US strikes.

  [^cite_note-799]

  [^cite_note-800]

- President Trump pledged to attack Iran "very hard", citing inadequate progress in war-ending negotiations.

  [^cite_note-801]

  &#x20;US ‌defense secretary Pete Hegseth said that ⁠the United States will strike Iran hard on ‌10 June and bomb "key facilities."

  [^cite_note-802]

  &#x20;The US military later launched strikes on multiple Iranian targets, firing 49 [Tomahawk missiles](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tomahawk_missiles) during the process.

  [^cite_note-803]

  [^cite_note-804]

- US forces attacked the tanker M/T *Settebello* (IMO 9162916) killing three Indian crew members. The US stated the crew had failed to comply with orders from American forces.

  [^cite_note-805]

- Drinking water facilities in [Bemani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bemani,_Iran), near the Strait of Hormuz, were damaged amid precision US strikes near the Strait. A local official reported that some 20,000 people lost access to water.

  [^cite_note-806]

### 11 June

- Iran declared the indefinite closure of the Strait of Hormuz and says that it fired at communication antennas and radar facilities of US 5th Fleet, American military bases in Kuwait and Bahrain and an American command center in Jordan in response to the US attacks.

  [^cite_note-807]

  [^cite_note-808]

  [^cite_note-809]

- Iranian attacks hit a key radar station on Bahrains highest mountain [Jabal al Dukhan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mountain_of_Smoke). Press research done by the [Neue Zürcher Zeitung](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neue_Z%C3%BCrcher_Zeitung) from mid June confirmed that the installation had been damaged.

  [^cite_note-810]

### 14 June

- Israel attacked the Dahiyeh district in Beirut, allegedly responding to a drone attack on targets in Israel earlier.

  [^cite_note-811]

### 21 June

- The first talks between the U.S. and Iran since the signing of the [Islamabad Memorandum](83441543.md) are held at the [Bürgenstock Resort](52151316.md) in [Switzerland](26748.md). The talks were mediated by [Pakistan](23235.md) and [Qatar](8238258.md).

  [^cite_note-812]

  [^cite_note-813]

- Five known ships pass through the Strait of Hormuz, down from 26 ships the previous day, amidst the re-closure.

  [^cite_note-814]

### 26 June

- Trump accused Iran of violating the truce with US by launching drone attacks on ships in the Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-815]

  &#x20;CENTCOM later announced that in retaliation to the alleged truce violations, the US hit Iranian missile and drone storage facilities, as well as coastal radar stations.

  [^cite_note-816]

  &#x20;Iran later stated it targeted American sites in the Gulf region in response to the attack.

  [^cite_note-817]

### 27 June

- The US military announced it struck Iranian military surveillance infrastructure, communication systems, air defense sites, drone storage facilities, and minelayer capabilities at the [commander-in-chief](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Commander-in-Chief_of_the_United_States)'s direction in retaliation to an alleged Iranian attack against a ship in the Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-818]

### 28 June

- Iran said it fired ballistic missiles and drones towards [the US Ali Al Salem Air Base](1469367.md) in Kuwait and [the US Fifth Fleet headquarters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Naval_Support_Activity_Bahrain) in Bahrain in retaliation to American attacks against five Iranian coastal sites.

  [^cite_note-819]

- IRGC forces and [Kurdistan Free Life Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kurdistan_Free_Life_Party) (PAK) militants clash at Gagesh Heights, resulting in the deaths of four PAK members.

  [^cite_note-kurds-820]

- The U.S. and Iran later agreed to stop exchange of attacks between them.

  [^cite_note-821]

## July

### 1 July

- IRGC soldiers ambush a [Democratic Party of Iranian Kurdistan](3197182.md) (KDPI) vehicle near [Piranshahr](30865712.md), killing six KDPI militants.

  [^cite_note-kurds-820]

### 7 July

- Iran reportedly launched at least two missiles at commercial vessels including Al Rekayyat, a [liquefied natural gas](832128.md) tanker owned by Nakilat QGTS.QA, part of Qatari LNG industry in the Hormuz, causing significant damage.

  [^cite_note-822]

  &#x20;Iranian state TV reported that one of those vessels was struck after ignoring warning, without directly claiming responsibility.

  [^cite_note-823]

  &#x20;Qatar accused Iran of carrying the attack against its gas tanker, describing it as a clear violation of [international law](8195726.md).

  [^cite_note-824]

  &#x20;On the same day, the UK military said that another tanker in the Hormuz was hit by an unidentified projectile.

  [^cite_note-825]

  &#x20;Americans announced that they revoked Iran oil sale and production deal in retaliation to the alleged Iranian attacks.

  [^cite_note-826]

  &#x20;Saudis condemned alleged Iranian attacks against one of their tankers and the Qatari tanker.

  [^cite_note-827]

  &#x20;Iran accused the US of violating [the memorandum that ended the war](83441543.md) with restoration of the oil sanctions.

  [^cite_note-828]

### 8 July

- The US Central Command announced that the US struck over 80 Iranian targets including [air defense systems](65797909.md), coastal radar sites and dozens of [IRGC](1285472.md) small boats to degrade Iranian capability to disrupt international maritime trade using precision munitions in retaliation to alleged Iranian truce violations.

  [^cite_note-829]

  [^cite_note-830]

  &#x20;Iran said that it shot down an American [MQ-9 drone](4769988.md) in southern Iran.

  [^cite_note-831]

  &#x20;On the same day, Iran announced that it targeted 85 American military targets in Bahrain, Kuwait in retaliation to alleged truce breaches.

  [^cite_note-832]

  &#x20;Iran later added that it targeted an American base in Bahrain.

  [^cite_note-833]

  &#x20;[IRGC Navy](4676345.md) announced that one of its soldiers was killed in a US drone attack.

  [^cite_note-834]

  &#x20;The same day, Trump declared that the MOU with Iran is "over" after Iran launched attacks on commercial vessels in the Strait of Hormuz to claim Iranian sovereignty over the Strait of Hormuz, startling US officials and shattering a brief diplomatic calm, adding that he did not want to continue dealing with the Iranian regime, which he referred to as “sick people.”

  [^cite_note-836]

  &#x20;He emphasized the need to "rid their cancer," advocating for early intervention akin to cutting out cancer.

  [^cite_note-837]

  [^cite_note-838]

  &#x20;On the same day, Trump secretly left Air Force One for a military jet via a catering container due to a threat from Iran.

  [^cite_note-840]

  [^cite_note-841]

  &#x20;Trump later stated that US negotiators can continue negotiations, although he expressed pessimism about the outcome.

  [^cite_note-842]

  &#x20;Iranian state media announced that eight service members from [the country's army](48259216.md) were killed in American attacks, adding that they served in [the air force](412792.md) and ⁠[navy](2160969.md) and died because of attacks ‌in ⁠[Bandar Abbas](464348.md) and [Bushehr](205669.md).

  [^cite_note-843]

  &#x20;The US Central Command announced that it hit roughly 90 Iranian military targets, including air defense systems, coastal surveillance assets, missile and drone storage facilities, naval capabilities, and military logistics infrastructure along the country's coastline.

  [^cite_note-844]

  [^cite_note-845]

### 9 July

- The IRGC said that it targeted two American bases in Kuwait and two in Bahrain.

  [^cite_note-846]

  &#x20;On the same day, Trump said that Iran contacted him and they want to make an agreement.

  [^cite_note-847]

  &#x20;The same day, the [Iranian army](48259216.md) said that it targeted American military sites in Kuwait, Qatar and Bahrain.

  [^cite_note-848]

  &#x20;[Iran's Health Ministry](24234168.md) said that 14 people were killed and 78 others were injured in American attacks in Iran.

  [^cite_note-849]

  &#x20;Iran accused the US of targeting civilian railway bridges in its strikes and described the Trump administration as "evil and psychopathic."

  [^cite_note-850]

  &#x20;[Fars News Agency](5000099.md) reported that the targeted bridges include a bridge on the China-Russia trade corridor in [Golestan province](448067.md), making it one of the deepest US strikes inside Iran since the war began, extending the target list beyond military sites in the south to trade infrastructure that the Iranian regime has relied on to circumvent [sanctions](25699056.md) and maintain overland links with China and Russia.

  [^cite_note-851]

  &#x20;Iranian media reported explosions in [Bushehr](205669.md), claiming the area around [the nuclear power plant](18522794.md) was affected.

  [^cite_note-852]

  [^cite_note-853]

  &#x20;American officials denied the claim, raising chance of involvement by a Gulf state.

  [^cite_note-854]

  [^cite_note-855]

  &#x20;Iran said that it targeted [Muwaffaq Salti Air Base](32592197.md).

  [^cite_note-856]

  &#x20;Iran also conducted drone strikes against an Iranian-Kurdish rebel group in the vicinity of Erbil, Iraq.

  [^cite_note-857]

  &#x20;Iranian state media reported that three IRGC soldiers were killed in the American attacks.

  [^cite_note-858]

### 11 July

- The IRGC Navy said that it closed the Strait until further notice after firing a warning shot at a vessel trying to transit through an unapproved route.

  [^cite_note-859]

### 12 July

- About 140 Iranian targets including missile and drone sites, ammunition storages, and communication networks were hit according to the US military.

  [^cite_note-860]

### 13 July

- Trump announced that the US will reinstate the "[Iranian blockade in the Strait of Hormuz](82937256.md)" and impose a 20% toll on cargo shipping through the strait.

  [^cite_note-861]

  &#x20;The Yemeni government and Saudi Arabia struck the [Sanaa International Airport](4202141.md) controlled by the Houthis,

  [^cite_note-862]

  [^cite_note-863]

  &#x20;allegedly to prevent an Iranian plane from landing.

  [^cite_note-864]

  &#x20;The Houthis retaliated by launching missiles and drones against Saudi Arabia's [Abha International Airport](9419420.md).

  [^cite_note-865]

  [^cite_note-866]

  &#x20;American led [Combined Maritime Forces](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Combined_Maritime_Forces)' Joint Maritime Information Centre (JMIC) announced that the US naval blockade on Iran will start the following day.

  [^cite_note-867]

  &#x20;The US announced that it hit military targets throughout Iran including Bushehr, [Chabahar](1658958.md), [Jask](19973566.md), [Konarak](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Konarak,_Iran), [Abu Musa](931858.md), and Bandar Abbas.

  [^cite_note-868]

  &#x20;Two Emirati tankers were reportedly struck by Iranian cruise missiles in the Strait in Oman's territorial waters, killing one Indian crew member and injuring 10 other Indian crews, two of them critically.

  [^cite_note-869]

  [^cite_note-870]

### 14 July

- The US military launched a new round of strikes against Iran in an effort to degrade capabilities used to attack commercial shipping in the Strait of Hormuz. Additionally, the U.S. Navy reinstated the [naval blockade](82937256.md) that ended on June 17 after the Islamabad MOU was signed.

  [^cite_note-871]

  &#x20;Despite this, Trump cancelled plans to impose fees in the strait.

  [^cite_note-872]

- Explosions were also reported in the Iranian cities of [Ahvaz](599079.md) and [Bandar Abbas](464348.md), and the islands of [Kish](847277.md) and [Qeshm](2252168.md).

  [^cite_note-873]

  &#x20;The IMO said that death toll from Hormuz ship attacks on that day increased to two.

  [^cite_note-874]

### 16 July

- The U.S. military launched strikes against Iran for a sixth consecutive day.

  [^cite_note-875]

  &#x20;Explosions were reported in Iran's [Qeshm Island](2252168.md), [Bushehr](205669.md), [Ahvaz](599079.md), and [Bandar Abbas](464348.md). A bridge in [Bandar Khamir](24750251.md) was targeted along with a truck, killing the driver.

  [^cite_note-876]

  &#x20;Additionally, [Tasnim News Agency](42800472.md) reported that [American fighter jets](75231243.md) attacked the [Iranshahr Airport](40057074.md) in the [Sistan and Baluchestan](448105.md) [province](448160.md).

  [^cite_note-877]

  [^cite_note-878]

  &#x20;Additionally, new videos were seen of strikes hitting the base.

  [^cite_note-879]

### 18 July

- The U.S. military reported that two American soldiers were killed in [Jordan](7515964.md) after [Iranian missiles attacked the country](82589203.md). In response, CENTCOM launched strikes against IRGC targets.

  [^cite_note-880]

  &#x20;The two troops were identified as 25-year-old Tyler James Feehan of [Hawaii](13270.md) and 19-year-old Isabella Gonzales of [Texas](29810.md).

  [^cite_note-881]

### 19 July

- The U.S. military reported that an American service member died in northern [Iraq](7515928.md), bringing the [total casualties](82723923.md#United_States) of American soldiers in the Iran war to 17.

  [^cite_note-882]

  [^cite_note-883]

  &#x20;Later in the evening, the U.S. launched strikes against Iran for a ninth consecutive evening.

  [^cite_note-884]

  [^cite_note-885]

### 20 July

- CENTCOM completed a 10th round of strikes against Iranian military command centers, missile and drone launch sites, maritime capabilities and air defense systems.

  [^cite_note-886]

- *[The New York Times](30680.md)* reported that American soldiers were injured and several [helicopters](42306263.md) were damaged during [Iranian strikes](82589203.md) against [Jordan](7515964.md).

  [^cite_note-887]

  [^cite_note-888]

  &#x20;Additionally, [Pentagon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Pentagon) [spokesperson](18433915.md) [Sean Parnell](81398395.md) said around 100 U.S. troops have been injured since the war resumed on July 7.

  [^cite_note-889]

### 21 July

- CENTCOM confirmed they had launched an 11th round of strikes against Iranian hangars and military targets such as logistics infrastructure.

  [^cite_note-890]

  [^cite_note-891]

  &#x20;Additionally, [Israeli finance minister](7088294.md) [Bezalel Smotrich](46196834.md) stated that Israel will not join the U.S. in striking Iran. An third American service member identified as Sgt. Angel S Rampersad is also confirmed to have been killed during an attack on the [Muwaffaq Salti Air Base](32592197.md) in Jordan.

  [^cite_note-892]

  &#x20;U.S. officials confirmed that a B-1 long-range bomber was used during a strike against [Islamic Revolutionary Guard Corps](1285472.md) targets.

  [^cite_note-893]

### 22 July

- [Tasnim News Agency](42800472.md) reported that at 2\:48 pm ([IRST](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iran_Standard_Time)), the U.S. military launched missile strikes against [Larak Island](2832280.md) in the Strait of Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-894]

  [^cite_note-895]

  &#x20;At 5\:30 p.m. ET, CENTCOM launches a new round of strikes against Iran, marking the 12th consecutive day of attacks against the country.

  [^cite_note-896]

  [^cite_note-897]

### 23 July

- The [Islamic Republic News Agency](841314.md) reported that the U.S. military struck the [Shalamcheh](8247289.md) area near the [Iran–Iraq border](10282111.md). Two people were reported to be killed and 11 were reported injured.

  [^cite_note-898]

  [^cite_note-899]

  &#x20;At 6\:45 p.m. ET, CENTCOM announced on [X](9988187.md) that they have conducted strikes against Iran for a 13th consecutive night amidst clashes over shipping routes, with explosions reported in [Ahvaz](599079.md), [Bandar Abbas](464348.md), and [Qeshm Island](2252168.md).

  [^cite_note-900]

  [^cite_note-901]

### 25 July

- The [Iranian health ministry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iranian_health_ministry) spokesperson Hossein Kermanpour reported no U.S. military strikes against Iran, after 13 consecutive nights of strikes by CENTCOM against the country.

  [^cite_note-902]

  &#x20;On 26 July, a senior official said that Iran will halt attacks as long as the U.S. does the same.

  [^cite_note-903]

  &#x20;Additionally, U.S. Ambassador to the UN [Mike Waltz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mike_Waltz) said that the U.S. paused the attacks to allow more room for diplomacy.

  [^cite_note-904]

  &#x20;Additionally it also marked the second consecutive day without strikes.

  [^cite_note-905]

### 29 July

- After six days of pause in strikes, CENTCOM at 8\:00pm launched retaliatory strikes against Iran in response to an attempted attack against Jordan, which was foiled the previous day.

  [^cite_note-906]

  &#x20;Earlier in the day, CENTCOM and [Saudi Arabian Armed Forces](27276.md) launched strikes against Iran-backed militias in Iraq that killed 20 [Popular Mobilization Forces](45671374.md) militants.

  [^cite_note-907]

  &#x20;Several members of the [Islamic Revolutionary Guard Corps](1285472.md) were also killed during the attacks.

  [^cite_note-908]

## August

### 1 August

- *[The New York Times](30680.md)* reported, citing state and local officials, that evidence leads to Iran in a series of cyberattacks against water systems in at least seven US states, with [Michigan](18859.md) being the second state to report the attacks publicly.

  [^cite_note-909]

### 2 August

- On 2 August, after [Qatari efforts to head off the attacks and open the Hormuz](76142543.md) and Saudi Crown Prince Mohammed bin Salman expressed concern about Trump's plans for massive strikes on Iran,

  [^cite_note-910]

  &#x20;Trump stated that he and Israel had called off the attack after the Iranian regime requested more time for a deal, and that a plan was in place for US forces to carry out the largest US operation since [World War II](32927.md).

  [^cite_note-911]

  [^cite_note-912]

  [^cite_note-913]

  &#x20;The [Islamic Republic of Iran Armed Forces](14305018.md) denied requesting the US to halt strikes, calling Trump's claim a "lie".

  [^cite_note-haaretz0208-914]

### 3 August

- Iran stated that there is no current negotiations with Americans, denying Trump's claims that negotiations with Iran would start that day.

  [^cite_note-915]

  [^cite_note-916]

- Iran is in talks with Oman, but the agreement is not enough to open the Strait of Hormuz, according to Foreign Ministry spokesman Esmail Baghaei.

  [^cite_note-917]

### 6 August

- Oman accepted the framework of an agreement with Iran to temporarily reopen the Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-918]

### 7 August

- [CNN](62028.md) reported that Gen. [Dan Caine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dan_Caine) has privately urged Trump advisers to seek an off-ramp from the war, warning that escalation could backfire and that airstrikes alone are unlikely to meet the administration's goals. While a decisive defeat of the Iranian regime would likely require a large ground operation, no one in the administration is currently pushing for that option.

  [^cite_note-919]

### 8 August

- The IRGC stated that reopening the Hormuz is not dependent on talks with Oman but rather on the US accepting Iran's conditions.

  [^cite_note-920]

- The UAE stated that Iran attacked an [Abu Dhabi National Oil Company](7992255.md) tanker in the Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-921]

- Iran's [Supreme National Security Council](6295948.md) outlined conditions for the US to fulfill before reopening the Hormuz including a commitment from the US to cease threats against Iran, end wars involving [non-state allies](36658251.md) like Hezbollah, Hamas and Houthis, lift the naval blockade of Iranian ports, withdraw US troops, compensate for losses from the [Twelve-Day War](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Twelve-Day_War) and the current war, remove all [sanctions](25699056.md), and release all [Iranian frozen assets](50371068.md).

  [^cite_note-922]

### 11 August

- US forces fired at the rudder of a Panama-flagged vessel that attempted to overcome its naval blockade of Iran while passing via the Gulf of Oman, without causing casualties.

  [^cite_note-923]

### 12 August

- [Abu Dhabi National Oil Company](7992255.md) stated that two of its vessels were attacked while passing through the Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-924]

### 13 August

- US defence secretary [Pete Hegseth](34076202.md) stated that the US can maintain a naval blockade on Iranian ports "indefinitely".

  [^cite_note-925]

### 14 August

- Iran stated that it downed an American UAV above [Hormozgan province](448064.md).

  [^cite_note-926]

- Two vessels, including one belonging to the Abu Dhabi National Oil Company were attacked while passing through the Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-927]

  [^cite_note-928]

### 16 August

- Iran Army Chief [Amir Hatami](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amir_Hatami) declared that the army is offering a bounty of \$30,000 for killing or capturing invading US soldiers, with the payment doubled if carried out by a woman.

  [^cite_note-929]

### 17 August

- The office of the [prime minister of the Kurdistan Region](26055397.md) was reportedly targeted by Iranian drones.

  [^cite_note-930]

  &#x20;Iran called the incident a "[false flag](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/False_flag)" attack.

  [^cite_note-931]

- Iran stated that it reached an agreement with Oman over the Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-932]

  &#x20;Trump threatened to bomb Oman — a longtime U.S. ally — for talking to Tehran about managing traffic in the Strait of Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-CNBC-933]

- Esmail Baghaei, Iran's Foreign Ministry spokesman, rejected talks on extending the MOU, state outlet [Tasnim](42800472.md) reported.

  [^cite_note-CNBC-933]

### 18 August

- The UAE stated that Iran fired two missile towards it, adding that one landed inside its territorial waters.

  [^cite_note-934]

  &#x20;The UAE then halted all trade, exchange, and financial [transactions](28045780.md) with Iran until further notice.

  [^cite_note-935]

  &#x20;Iran denied that it launched missiles towards the UAE, citing a "false flag" operation.

  [^cite_note-936]

  &#x20;Analysts from maritime intelligence firm Kpler told [CNN](62028.md) on that Iran looks to have lost control of the strait partially and is unable to impose tolls due to increased usage of the U.N.-authorized Omani transit route.

  [^cite_note-937]

  [^cite_note-938]

### 19 August

- Iran declared that if Trump escalated the war, it could strike military targets in Europe. [NATO](21133.md) said it is prepared to defend its members.

  [^cite_note-939]

  [^cite_note-940]

### 20 August

- Declaring "economic "[economic D-day](84050791.md)," Trump announced that any nation trading with Iran would be sanctioned.

  [^cite_note-941]

  &#x20;[Mohsen Rezaee](1862765.md), the secretary of Iran's National Security Council, stated that any regional country that participates in the US sanctions against Iran will be regarded as an enemy.

  [^cite_note-aljazeera.com-942]

  &#x20;The Asia-based [USS *George Washington*](198834.md) replaced [*Abraham Lincoln*](199106.md), resulting in the lack of a US aircraft carrier in the [Indo-Pacific](330456.md).

  [^cite_note-945]

  [^cite_note-946]

  &#x20;On 20 July, the Houthis announced [a blockade of Saudi Arabian ports and ships](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Houthi_blockade_of_Saudi_Arabia) in the [Strait of Bab al-Mandab](62138.md).

  [^cite_note-AJ-23072026-947]

### 22 August

- Iraq secured Iranian authorization for a number of its oil tankers to transit through the Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-aljazeera.com-942]

### 24 August

- Iran announced it blacklisted 45 tankers which have violated its rules for passing through the Hormuz, and would take action against any vessels transferring loads with them.

  [^cite_note-948]

- US Treasury Secretary [Scott Bessent](38532044.md) announced a new US sanctions campaign against the Iranian regime dubbed *Operation Economic Outcast*, warning that every country has a set deadline to halt economic activities with the regime identified by Washington or risk Treasury action which includes that removal of the [US dollar](18717338.md) financial system.

  [^cite_note-949]

  [^cite_note-950]

### 25 August

- US officials with firsthand knowledge corroborated Trump's assertion that the US Navy had cleared mines from the Hormuz Traffic Separation Scheme, Oman's primary maritime corridor with Iran, adding that over the last few months, underwater drones carried out a systematic scanning of the area, identifying over 100 objects suspected of having mines, which were then removed or detonated by private contractors.

  [^cite_note-951]

  &#x20;Trump added that the US will destroy Iranian vessels laying more mines in the Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-952]

- US Secretary of State [Marco Rubio](5502549.md) told allies around the globe that "for the time being," the US is not expected to resume major combat operations against the Iranian regime, with a second US official adding that this is expected to be the policy at least until after the [midterm election](70357296.md), when a new military campaign could be considered.

  [^cite_note-953]

### 26 August

- The IRGC announced that Iran and Oman reached deals on the Hormuz share and its revenue.

  [^cite_note-954]

### 27 August

- Maritime intelligence firm Kplr confirmed declining Iranian control in the Strait of Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-955]

### 30 August

- A recent Pentagon assessment warned Defense Secretary Pete Hegseth that sustained military operations against Iran could leave the U.S. less able to respond to threats elsewhere and protect the homeland, although Chief Pentagon Spokesman Sean Parnell dismissed the report as inaccurate.

  [^cite_note-956]

- US forces stated that they targeted two Iranian launchers on [Larak Island](2832280.md) after IRGC forces were identified preparing to launch rockets carrying sea mines toward the Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-957]

  &#x20;The IRGC stated that the attack killed and injured several Iranian soldiers and civilians.

  [^cite_note-958]

### 31 August

- Iran claimed to have retaliated by attacking U.S. bases in Jordan and U.S. military assets in the United Arab Emirates.

  [^cite_note-959]

  &#x20;It also shot down a U.S. MQ-9 drone in the Strait of Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-960]

  &#x20;Trump promised to respond to the Iranian attack against American soldiers in Jordan.

  [^cite_note-961]

  &#x20;Iranian media stated that a Saudi oil tanker was stopped while transiting the southern corridor of the Strait of Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-962]

## September

### 1 September

- The British military announced that a tanker was hit by three projectiles while getting out of the Strait of Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-963]

- Following a series of reciprocal military strikes between the United States and Iran, crude oil prices rose above the \$90-per-barrel level, driven by market perceptions of escalating geopolitical risk.

  [^cite_note-964]

- The US announced that it struck IRGC targets in Iran, adding that it targeted tankers owned by the [National Iranian Oil Company](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Iranian_Oil_Company) as part of a new "tanker for tanker" policy.

  [^cite_note-965]

  [^cite_note-966]

  &#x20;Trump said that one target was Iranian work to rebuild radar systems.

  [^cite_note-967]

  &#x20;Iranian state media said that US targets included [Jiroft Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jiroft_Airport),

  [^cite_note-968]

  &#x20;and that [a US strike](84122275.md) in [Kuhestak](37892128.md) killed five people including two children and injured over 63 others in a wedding;

  [^cite_note-969]

  [^cite_note-timesofisrael.com-970]

  [^cite_note-971]

  &#x20;it announced new attacks against US targets in the region.

  [^cite_note-972]

  &#x20;A communications tower about 130 metres (430 ft) south of the wedding location was also struck in the attack.

  [^cite_note-bbc-20260902a-973]

  [^cite_note-ii0209-974]

  &#x20;In response to the Kuhestak attack, Iran targeted Camp Titin, a [Marine Corps](17349325.md) base near the [Gulf of Aqaba](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gulf_of_Aqaba), and [Prince Hassan Air Base](37363389.md) in Jordan (home to [RQ-4](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Northrop_Grumman_RQ-4_Global_Hawk) and [MQ-9](4769988.md) drones), which Iran said resulted in the destruction of several aircraft and the killing of a number of American personnel. Jordan announced that it had shot down 10 of 13 missiles launched at its territory, with the rest hitting open ground.

  [^cite_note-975]

  [^cite_note-976]

- Iran announced new attacks against US targets in the region.

  [^cite_note-timesofisrael.com-970]

  &#x20;A communications tower in Kuhestak was hit in a US attack.

  [^cite_note-ii0209-974]

### 2 September

- The Deputy Governor of [Khuzestan Province](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Khuzestan_Province) for Security and Law Enforcement Affairs stated that a US missile attack on three areas in the province killed seven people and wounded eight others the day before.

  [^cite_note-977]

- The IRGC announced that its four aerospace personnel and three [Basij](2892082.md) members were killed in separate US attacks in [Kermanshah province](448078.md) and [Lavan Island](1954991.md) the previous day, adding that several other Basij members were injured in the attack on Lavan Island.

  [^cite_note-978]

  [^cite_note-979]

- Donald Trump posts on [Truth Social](69077351.md) about changing the name of the Hormuz Strait to the "Trump Strait".

  [^cite_note-980]

- Iran stated that a total of 18 people were killed and 108 others were injured in US attacks the prior day.

  [^cite_note-981]

- US Defense Secretary [Pete Hegseth](34076202.md) extended the deployments of approximately 50,000 American soldiers in West Asia, which include warship crews, air defense units, and paratroopers, until 2027.

  [^cite_note-982]

### 3 September

- Iran announced that it targeted [Ahmad al-Jaber Air Base](5306887.md) and [Al Minhad Air Base](29156193.md).

  [^cite_note-983]

- Iran media declared that six navy soldiers were killed in a US attack in south two days before.

  [^cite_note-984]

- US Vice President [JD Vance](53396477.md) announced that Washington cut contact with Iran as long as it fires at vessels traveling through the Strait of Hormuz without its permission.

  [^cite_note-985]

- The Houthis launched [a general offensive](84167596.md) in coastal regions for control of the [Bab-el-Mandeb Strait](62138.md).

  [^cite_note-986]

### 4 September

- The US military announced that it had disabled ad trackers on devices following reports that commercially available location data had been used to target its soldiers in the Mideast.

  [^cite_note-987]

### 5 September

- The IRGC announced that it struck a US aircraft carrier and a navy destroyer with ballistic missiles, saying both vessels were damaged and forced to leave the area. The US military declared that those two vessels evaded the attack.

  [^cite_note-988]

- The US military reported that it destroyed three IRGC-connected oil tankers in response to latter's fire on its warships.

  [^cite_note-989]

- The IRGC stated that it targeted three oil tankers which were traversing through Hormuz without its approval, adding that it launched strikes on three additional US vessels in other areas following the US attacks.

  [^cite_note-990]

### 6 September

- The IRGC declared that it struck a US [drone boat](7808557.md) which attempted to enter Hormuz, a claim rejected by the US as a "total lie".

  [^cite_note-991]

  [^cite_note-992]

### 7 September

- Lebanon's health ministry announced that at least 4,362 people had been killed in Israeli attacks since 2 March 2026, including 11 in the past 24 hours.

  [^cite_note-993]

- Iran's top negotiator, Mohammad Bagher Ghalibaf, warned that U.S. attacks will trigger a faster, heavier, and more painful response, saying the "rules of the game have changed."

  [^cite_note-994]

### 8 September

- Iranian army announced that [air defence systems](65797909.md) downed a [General Atomics MQ-1 Predator](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/General_Atomics_MQ-1_Predator) drone above [Bandar Abbas](464348.md).

  [^cite_note-995]

- The Revolutionary Guards also announced that they had seized a US-made Dive-LD unmanned submarine at the entrance to the Strait of Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-996]

### 9 September

- The US announced that it destroyed five Iranian tankers, one of which was sunk, in retaliation to additional attempted missile attacks on one of its Navy warships. Jordan said that it intercepted 18 of 20 ballistic missiles coming from Iran, adding that two missiles landed in uninhabited regions. The IRGC stated that it struck two US vessels and eight oil tankers in Hormuz following the US attacks.

  [^cite_note-997]

  [^cite_note-998]

  [^cite_note-999]

  [^cite_note-1000]

  &#x20;The IRGC claim related to the US vessels was denied by the US.

  [^cite_note-1001]

  &#x20;Iran announced that it targeted US military sites in Jordan.

  [^cite_note-1002]

  &#x20;The British military announced that several merchant vessels in the northern Gulf and the [Gulf of Oman](12896.md) were struck with disabling fire during military activity there.

  [^cite_note-1003]

  &#x20;Panama-flagged tanker *New Andros* was attacked with UAV in Iraqi waters.

  [^cite_note-1004]

  &#x20;One crew member was killed and one person was left unaccounted for after an event involving oil tanker *Hercules Star* near Dubai.

  [^cite_note-1005]

  &#x20;A [Fairchild Republic A-10 Thunderbolt II](12502446.md) was hit and left with a missing wing, while approximately eight [F-15s](11715.md) were lightly damaged in the Iranian strike on [Muwaffaq Salti Air Base](32592197.md).

  [^cite_note-1006]

### 11 September

- The [Houthis](17240332.md) recaptured the island of [Perim](1907645.md), opening a new front in the war.

  [^cite_note-1007]

  [^cite_note-1008]

  &#x20;The Houthis completed their takeover of the Bab el-Mandeb area after capturing the island, which sits in the middle of the shipping lane and divides the strait into two navigable channels.

  [^cite_note-1009]

- Saudi Crown Prince [Mohammed bin Salman](38689358.md) reportedly urged President Trump to take military action against the Houthis, but Trump declined. He later said the Houthis had signaled that they did not want a conflict with the United States.

  [^cite_note-1010]

- Trump explicitly framed the war as a continuation of the post-9/11 [War on Terror](13425800.md).

  [^cite_note-1011]

- Saudi Arabia announced that it has temporarily closed the [East–West Crude Oil Pipeline](54166263.md) in the [Riyadh](51554.md) and [Medina](36636.md) areas because of attacks by UAVs from Iraq the day before, adding that some people were wounded.

  [^cite_note-1012]

  [^cite_note-1013]

  [^cite_note-1014]

  &#x20;The pipeline is one of the kingdom's main routes for exporting crude while bypassing the Strait of Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-trump-pipeline-1015]

### 12 September

- The US restricted air defence time slots for vessels traversing Hormuz, limiting guaranteed military protection to two specific time windows per day instead of continuous overnight coverage, after increased Iranian attacks on ships travelling at night.

  [^cite_note-1016]

  [^cite_note-1017]

- Trump stated that Iran was "probably responsible" for the aerial attack on Saudi Arabia that shut down the East–West pipeline, and said he had spoken with Saudi Crown Prince [Mohammed bin Salman](38689358.md).

  [^cite_note-trump-pipeline-1015]

  [^cite_note-1018]

- IRGC and Iranian police forces engaged in an [hours-long conflict with militants of the PFF](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sistan_and_Balochistan_clashes_\(2026%E2%80%93present\)) in Saravan, resulting in the deaths of four Baloch separatists and multiple IRGC soldiers.

  [^cite_note-1019]

### 14 September

- Missiles targeted three Iranian Kurdish opposition camps in [Sulaymaniyah](653537.md).

  [^cite_note-1020]

- Trump announced that he is willing to engage with Iran to negotiate a deal.

  [^cite_note-1021]

- [Fars News Agency](5000099.md) reported, citing the IRGC that a vessel exploded and caught fire following collision with mines in Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-1022]

- A U.S. Department of Defense report indicates that the Iran war costs reached approximately \$33.4 billion by late June. Furthermore, high munitions usage has depleted strategic reserves and exposed critical bottlenecks in the industrial capacity needed for resupply.

  [^cite_note-1023]

### 15 September

- The Iranian government rejected any negotiations with the US until its demands are met.

  [^cite_note-1024]

- The US military announced that it destroyed two Iranian small boats the day before following an IRGC attempt to capture one of its Navy drones patrolling Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-1025]

- Fars News Agency reported that the IRGC intercepted a MQ-1 drone above Hormuz in the third such incident on that day.

  [^cite_note-1026]

### 16 September

- According to the US Congressional Budget Office, the United States had spent over \$38 billion (€33 billion) on its war with Iran between the conflict's start on 28 February and 1 August.

  [^cite_note-1027]

### 17 September

- In an interview with *Axios*, Trump stated that he faces a "big decision" on whether to resume large-scale military attacks against the Iranian regime or pursue a diplomatic agreement to end the almost seven-month-old conflict. Trump announced that he would consult with leaders from six Gulf Cooperation Council states—Saudi Arabia, the UAE, Qatar, Bahrain, Kuwait, and Oman—on the sidelines of the upcoming UN General Assembly meeting in New York to gauge regional alignment before finalizing a post-war strategy.

  [^cite_note-1028]

- The British military reported a security event in Hormuz, near Oman.

  [^cite_note-1029]

- The US confirmed that Iran shot down at least two of its MQ-1 drones in the past few days.

  [^cite_note-1030]

- Iranian personnel and People's Fighters Front militants clash at [Zahedan](1044089.md), with casualties reported for both sides.

  [^cite_note-1031]

### 18 September

- The IRGC stated that it hit a Togo-flagged vessel the day before while trying to make an "illegal passage" through Hormuz, adding that it came to a halt after catching fire.

  [^cite_note-1032]

- The British military stated that a vessel was hit by an "unknown projectile" in Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-1033]

- Hundreds of thousands rallied in Iran in a government-organized show of defiance — the largest demonstration since the US and Israel attacked in February — vowing to fight.

  [^cite_note-1034]

### 19 September

- The US Central Command commander Adm. [Brad Cooper](67555995.md) stated that CENTCOM forces enabled more than one billion barrels of crude oil to exit the Gulf through Hormuz in recent months.

  [^cite_note-1035]

- Iran stated that it has given mediators conditions such as ending all wars against [its non state allies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Axis_of_resistance), unfreezing Iranian funds, and lifting the [naval blockade](82937256.md) for negotiations with the US to end the war.

  [^cite_note-1036]

### 20 September

- The Liberia-flagged vessel *Al Maryah* was attacked while getting out of Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-1037]

### 21 September

- An attack on a tanker in Hormuz slightly injured two crew members.

  [^cite_note-1038]

### 23 September

- An attack on a cargo vessel in Hormuz inflicted two casualties.

  [^cite_note-1039]

- Iran stated that the US should accept to a “safe shipping route” through Hormuz which is determined by Iran and Oman for negotiations to even start.

  [^cite_note-1040]

  [^cite_note-1041]

- An Indian crew member was killed in an attack on Antigua and Barbuda-flagged cargo ship *Cape Dao* in Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-1042]

- Iranian personnel and PFF militants exchange gunfire at the town of Jihadabad. Multiple PFF fighters and Iranian personnel were reported killed including senior IRGC commander Hossein Zarifi and IRGC base intelligence chief Ahmad Herati Zeraati per Tasnim.

  [^cite_note-1043]

### 26 September

- Trump rejected Iran's seven-day ceasefire proposal and expressed doubt that Tehran would eventually comply with his administration's [nuclear](721807.md) demands.

  [^cite_note-1044]

  [^cite_note-1045]

### 27 September

- The IRGC stated that it seized a US [“Remus 600” autonomous submarine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/REMUS_\(vehicle\)) from Hormuz, a claim denied by the US.

  [^cite_note-1046]

  [^cite_note-1047]

- Trump said that he still expected more talks with Iran to resume during the week.

  [^cite_note-IndiaTodaySep2026-1048]

  &#x20;Trump claimed that Iran "wanted to make a deal," but they "overplayed their hand" by offering conditions that the US "would have maybe agreed to a year ago."

  [^cite_note-IndiaTodaySep2026-1048]

  [^cite_note-1049]

### 29 September

- Greek maritime risk management and intelligence firm Marisks announced that three vessels were struck by projectiles in Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-1050]

## October

### 1 October

- The US military started sending about 9,000 soldiers to the Middle East on naval ships, such as the [USS *Makin Island*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/USS_Makin_Island_\(LHD-8\)) amphibious readiness group and the [USS *Theodore Roosevelt*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/USS_Theodore_Roosevelt_\(CVN-71\)) carrier strike group.

  [^cite_note-1051]

- The [Fars News Agency](5000099.md) announced that a supertanker was struck eight km from Omani coast while crossing Hormuz “illegally”.

  [^cite_note-1052]

  &#x20;This was later confirmed by the British military.

  [^cite_note-1053]

### 2 October

- The UK military announced that a tanker was attacked by an unidentified projectile while traversing outbound through Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-1054]

### 3 October

- The UKTMO stated that two tankers were attacked by projectiles of unknown origin in two different incidents in Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-1055]

Iran fired six ballistic missiles and 29 drones at Saudi Arabia's Prince Sultan air base in a Friday attack that injured at least 15 troops, including five seriously, according to the people who were not authorized to comment publicly and spoke on the condition of anonymity. The base had come under come attack twice earlier this week, including an incident that injured 14 U.S. troops, according to the people who had been briefed on the matter. Located about 96 kilometers (60 miles) from the Saudi capital of Riyadh, the base is run by the Royal Saudi Air Force, but also used by U.S. troops.
